+// The CPU octree is flattened in depth-first order. A failed box test jumps
+// directly to the first node after the subtree, avoiding device recursion.
+struct DeviceNode {
+    float3 center, half;
+    int first, count, end;

+static void flattenOctree(const Octree& node, std::vector<DeviceNode>& nodes,
+                          std::vector<int>& indices) {
+    int pos = static_cast<int>(nodes.size());
+    nodes.push_back({make_float3(node.center.x, node.center.y, node.center.z),
+                     make_float3(node.halfExtent.x, node.halfExtent.y, node.halfExtent.z),
+                     static_cast<int>(indices.size()),
+                     static_cast<int>(node.triangleIndices.size()), 0});
+    for (size_t idx : node.triangleIndices) indices.push_back(static_cast<int>(idx));
+    for (const auto& child : node.children) {
+        if (child) flattenOctree(*child, nodes, indices);
+    }
+    nodes[pos].end = static_cast<int>(nodes.size());
+}

+static void cudaCheck(cudaError_t status) {

+        fprintf(stderr, "CUDA error: %s\n", cudaGetErrorString(status));
+        std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);

+__device__ __forceinline__ float3 add3(float3 a, float3 b) {
+    return make_float3(a.x+b.x, a.y+b.y, a.z+b.z);
+}
+__device__ __forceinline__ float3 sub3(float3 a, float3 b) {
+    return make_float3(a.x-b.x, a.y-b.y, a.z-b.z);
+}
+__device__ __forceinline__ float3 mul3(float3 a, float b) {
+    return make_float3(a.x*b, a.y*b, a.z*b);
+}
+__device__ __forceinline__ float dot3(float3 a, float3 b) {
+    return a.x*b.x + a.y*b.y + a.z*b.z;
+}
+__device__ __forceinline__ float3 cross3(float3 a, float3 b) {
+    return make_float3(a.y*b.z-a.z*b.y, a.z*b.x-a.x*b.z, a.x*b.y-a.y*b.x);
+}
+__device__ __forceinline__ float3 vec3(const Vec3& a) {
+    return make_float3(a.x, a.y, a.z);
+}
+__device__ __forceinline__ float3 randomPoint(const Triangle& tri, unsigned& seed) {
+    seed ^= seed << 13; seed ^= seed >> 17; seed ^= seed << 5;
+    float u = static_cast<float>(seed >> 8) * 0x1p-24f;
+    seed ^= seed << 13; seed ^= seed >> 17; seed ^= seed << 5;
+    float v = static_cast<float>(seed >> 8) * 0x1p-24f;
+    if (u + v > 1.0f) { u = 1.0f-u; v = 1.0f-v; }
+    float3 a = vec3(tri.a);
+    return add3(a, add3(mul3(sub3(vec3(tri.b),a),u),
+                        mul3(sub3(vec3(tri.c),a),v)));
+}
+
+__device__ __forceinline__ bool rayBox(float3 p1, float3 p2, const DeviceNode& node) {
+    float3 d = mul3(sub3(p2,p1),0.5f);
+    float3 c = sub3(add3(p1,d),node.center);
+    float3 h = node.half;
+    float ax=fabsf(d.x), ay=fabsf(d.y), az=fabsf(d.z);
+    if (fabsf(c.x)>h.x+ax || fabsf(c.y)>h.y+ay || fabsf(c.z)>h.z+az) return false;
+    if (fabsf(d.y*c.z-d.z*c.y)>h.y*az+h.z*ay+EPSILON) return false;
+    if (fabsf(d.z*c.x-d.x*c.z)>h.z*ax+h.x*az+EPSILON) return false;
+    if (fabsf(d.x*c.y-d.y*c.x)>h.x*ay+h.y*ax+EPSILON) return false;
+    return true;
+}
+
+__device__ __forceinline__ bool rayTriangle(float3 orig, float3 dir,
+                                              const Triangle& tri, float maxDist) {
+    float3 v0=vec3(tri.a), e1=sub3(vec3(tri.b),v0), e2=sub3(vec3(tri.c),v0);
+    float3 pvec=cross3(dir,e2);
+    float det=dot3(e1,pvec);
+    if (fabsf(det)<EPSILON) return false;
+    float invDet=1.0f/det;
+    float3 tvec=sub3(orig,v0);
+    float u=dot3(tvec,pvec)*invDet;
+    if (u<0.0f || u>1.0f) return false;
+    float3 qvec=cross3(tvec,e1);
+    float v=dot3(dir,qvec)*invDet;
+    if (v<0.0f || u+v>1.0f) return false;
+    float dist=dot3(e2,qvec)*invDet;
+    return dist>EPSILON && dist<maxDist-EPSILON;
+}
+
+__device__ bool blocked(float3 p1, float3 p2, const Triangle* tris,
+                        const DeviceNode* nodes, const int* indices,
+                        int src, int dst) {
+    float3 d=sub3(p2,p1);
+    float len=sqrtf(dot3(d,d));
+    if (len<EPSILON) return true;
+    float3 dir=mul3(d,1.0f/len);
+    int nodeId=0;
+    while (nodeId<nodes[0].end) {
+        DeviceNode node=nodes[nodeId];
+        if (nodeId && !rayBox(p1,p2,node)) { nodeId=node.end; continue; }
+        for (int k=0;k<node.count;++k) {
+            int idx=indices[node.first+k];
+            if (idx!=src && idx!=dst && rayTriangle(p1,dir,tris[idx],len)) return true;
+        }
+        ++nodeId;
+    }
+    return false;
+}
+
+__global__ void precomputeKernel(const Triangle* tris, const DeviceNode* nodes,
+                                 const int* indices, float* kij, int* tau, int n) {
+    size_t pair=static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x)*blockDim.x+threadIdx.x;
+    size_t total=static_cast<size_t>(n)*n;
+    if (pair>=total) return;
+    int i=static_cast<int>(pair/n), j=static_cast<int>(pair%n);
+    if (i==j) { kij[pair]=0.0f; tau[pair]=0; return; }
+    const Triangle& a=tris[i];
+    const Triangle& b=tris[j];
+    float3 delta=sub3(mul3(add3(add3(vec3(a.a),vec3(a.b)),vec3(a.c)),1.0f/3.0f),
+                      mul3(add3(add3(vec3(b.a),vec3(b.b)),vec3(b.c)),1.0f/3.0f));
+    tau[pair]=static_cast<int>(ceilf(sqrtf(dot3(delta,delta))*INV_WAVE_SPEED));
+    float3 na=vec3(a._normal), nb=vec3(b._normal);
+    if (dot3(na,nb)>0.99f) { kij[pair]=0.0f; return; }
+    unsigned seed=static_cast<unsigned>(pair) ^ 0x9e3779b9u;
+    seed ^= seed >> 16; seed *= 0x7feb352du;
+    seed ^= seed >> 15; seed *= 0x846ca68bu;
+    seed ^= seed >> 16;
+    if (!seed) seed=1;
+    float sum=0.0f;
+    for (int r=0;r<NUM_RAYS;++r) {
+        float3 pI=randomPoint(a,seed), pJ=randomPoint(b,seed);
+        if (blocked(pI,pJ,tris,nodes,indices,i,j)) continue;
+        float3 v=sub3(pJ,pI);
+        float ds=dot3(v,v);
+        if (ds<EPSILON) continue;
+        float invLen=rsqrtf(ds);
+        float ci=fmaxf(0.0f,dot3(v,na)*invLen);
+        float cj=fmaxf(0.0f,-dot3(v,nb)*invLen);
+        if (ci>0.0f && cj>0.0f) sum+=(ci*cj)/(PI*ds);
+    }
+    kij[pair]=sum*INV_NUM_RAYS;
+}
+
+__global__ void propagateKernel(const float* kij, const int* tau, const float* area,
+                                float* radB, int n, int t, int source, int timeOff,
+                                float rho) {
+    int lane=threadIdx.x&31;
+    int i=(blockIdx.x*blockDim.x+threadIdx.x)>>5;
+    if (i>=n) return;
+    float sum=0.0f;
+    size_t row=static_cast<size_t>(i)*n;
+    for (int j=lane;j<n;j+=32) {
+        int delay=tau[row+j];
+        if (i==j || t<delay) continue;
+        float k=kij[row+j];
+        if (k<=0.0f) continue;
+        float rad=radB[static_cast<size_t>(t-delay)*n+j];
+        if (rad>0.0f) sum+=fminf(k*area[j],1.0f)*rad;
+    }
+    for (int offset=16;offset>0;offset>>=1)
+        sum+=__shfl_down_sync(0xffffffff,sum,offset);
+    if (lane==0) radB[static_cast<size_t>(t)*n+i]=rho*sum+(i==source && t<timeOff ? 1.0f : 0.0f);
+}
+
+__global__ void correlateKernel(const float* radB, float* corr, int n, int steps,
+                                int source) {
+    size_t pair=static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x)*blockDim.x+threadIdx.x;
+    if (pair>=static_cast<size_t>(n)*steps) return;
+    int i=static_cast<int>(pair/steps), lag=static_cast<int>(pair%steps);
+    float sum=0.0f;
+    for (int tt=lag;tt<steps;++tt)
+        sum+=radB[static_cast<size_t>(tt-lag)*n+source]*radB[static_cast<size_t>(tt)*n+i];
+    corr[pair]=sum;
+}
+
+__global__ void distanceKernel(const float* corr, float* distances, int n, int steps) {
+    int i=blockIdx.x*blockDim.x+threadIdx.x;
+    if (i>=n) return;
+    float best=0.0f;
+    int bestT=0;
+    for (int t=0;t<steps;++t) {
+        float v=corr[static_cast<size_t>(i)*steps+t];
+        if (v>best) { best=v; bestT=t; }
+    }
+    distances[i]=WAVE_SPEED*bestT;
+}
+
+struct GpuState {
+    Triangle* tris=nullptr;
+    DeviceNode* nodes=nullptr;
+    int* indices=nullptr;
+    float* area=nullptr;
+    float* kij=nullptr;
+    int* tau=nullptr;
+    float* radB=nullptr;
+    float* corr=nullptr;
+    float* distances=nullptr;
+    int n, steps, source;
+    float rho;
+
+    explicit GpuState(const SimulationState& state)
+        : n(static_cast<int>(state.numTriangles)),
+          steps(static_cast<int>(state.numTimesteps)),
+          source(static_cast<int>(state.sourceIndex)), rho(state.rho[0]) {
+        std::vector<DeviceNode> hostNodes;
+        std::vector<int> hostIndices;
+        flattenOctree(state.octree,hostNodes,hostIndices);
+        auto allocCopy=[](auto** dst, const auto& src) {
+            cudaCheck(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(dst),src.size()*sizeof(*src.data())));
+            cudaCheck(cudaMemcpy(*dst,src.data(),src.size()*sizeof(*src.data()),cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+        };
+        allocCopy(&tris,state.triangles);
+        allocCopy(&nodes,hostNodes);
+        allocCopy(&indices,hostIndices);
+        allocCopy(&area,state.areas);
+        size_t pairs=static_cast<size_t>(n)*n;
+        cudaCheck(cudaMalloc(&kij,pairs*sizeof(float)));
+        cudaCheck(cudaMalloc(&tau,pairs*sizeof(int)));
+        cudaCheck(cudaMalloc(&radB,static_cast<size_t>(n)*steps*sizeof(float)));
+        cudaCheck(cudaMemset(radB,0,static_cast<size_t>(n)*steps*sizeof(float)));
+        cudaCheck(cudaMalloc(&corr,static_cast<size_t>(n)*steps*sizeof(float)));
+        cudaCheck(cudaMalloc(&distances,static_cast<size_t>(n)*sizeof(float)));
+    }
+    ~GpuState() {
+        cudaFree(tris); cudaFree(nodes); cudaFree(indices); cudaFree(area);
+        cudaFree(kij); cudaFree(tau); cudaFree(radB); cudaFree(corr); cudaFree(distances);
+    }
+    void precompute() {
+        printf("Computing time delays (Tau)...\nComputing form factors (Kij)...\n");
+        size_t pairs=static_cast<size_t>(n)*n;
+        precomputeKernel<<<static_cast<unsigned>((pairs+255)/256),256>>>(tris,nodes,indices,kij,tau,n);
+        cudaCheck(cudaGetLastError());
+        cudaCheck(cudaDeviceSynchronize());
+    }
+    void simulate() {
+        printf("Running wave propagation simulation...\n");
+        for (int t=0;t<steps;++t) {
+            propagateKernel<<<(n*32+127)/128,128>>>(kij,tau,area,radB,n,t,source,steps/2,rho);
+            cudaCheck(cudaGetLastError());
+            if ((t+1)%10==0 || t+1==steps) printf("  Timestep %d/%d\n",t+1,steps);
+        }
+        cudaCheck(cudaDeviceSynchronize());
+    }
+    void computeDistances(SimulationState& state) {
+        printf("Computing distances via cross-correlation...\n");
+        size_t pairs=static_cast<size_t>(n)*steps;
+        correlateKernel<<<static_cast<unsigned>((pairs+255)/256),256>>>(radB,corr,n,steps,source);
+        cudaCheck(cudaGetLastError());
+        distanceKernel<<<(n+255)/256,256>>>(corr,distances,n,steps);
+        cudaCheck(cudaGetLastError());
+        cudaCheck(cudaDeviceSynchronize());
+        cudaCheck(cudaMemcpy(state.distances.data(),distances,n*sizeof(float),cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));
+        cudaCheck(cudaMemcpy(state.radB.data(),radB,pairs*sizeof(float),cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));
+        cudaCheck(cudaMemcpy(state.kij.data(),kij,static_cast<size_t>(n)*n*sizeof(float),cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));
+        cudaCheck(cudaMemcpy(state.tau.data(),tau,static_cast<size_t>(n)*n*sizeof(int),cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));
+    }
+};
+
 // ============================================================================
 // Precomputation Phase
 // ============================================================================

+// The CPU octree is flattened in depth-first order. A failed box test jumps
+// directly to the first node after the subtree, avoiding device recursion.
+struct DeviceNode {
+    float3 center, half;
+    int first, count, end;
+};
+
+static void flattenOctree(const Octree& node, std::vector<DeviceNode>& nodes,
+                          std::vector<int>& indices) {
+    int pos = static_cast<int>(nodes.size());
+    nodes.push_back({make_float3(node.center.x, node.center.y, node.center.z),
+                     make_float3(node.halfExtent.x, node.halfExtent.y, node.halfExtent.z),
+                     static_cast<int>(indices.size()),
+                     static_cast<int>(node.triangleIndices.size()), 0});
+    for (size_t idx : node.triangleIndices) indices.push_back(static_cast<int>(idx));
+    for (const auto& child : node.children) {
+        if (child) flattenOctree(*child, nodes, indices);
+    }
+    nodes[pos].end = static_cast<int>(nodes.size());
+}
+
+static void cudaCheck(cudaError_t status) {
+    if (status != cudaSuccess) {
+        fprintf(stderr, "CUDA error: %s\n", cudaGetErrorString(status));
+        std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);
+    }
+}
+
+__device__ __forceinline__ float3 add3(float3 a, float3 b) {
+    return make_float3(a.x+b.x, a.y+b.y, a.z+b.z);
+}
+__device__ __forceinline__ float3 sub3(float3 a, float3 b) {
+    return make_float3(a.x-b.x, a.y-b.y, a.z-b.z);
+}
+__device__ __forceinline__ float3 mul3(float3 a, float b) {
+    return make_float3(a.x*b, a.y*b, a.z*b);
+}
+__device__ __forceinline__ float dot3(float3 a, float3 b) {
+    return a.x*b.x + a.y*b.y + a.z*b.z;
+}
+__device__ __forceinline__ float3 cross3(float3 a, float3 b) {
+    return make_float3(a.y*b.z-a.z*b.y, a.z*b.x-a.x*b.z, a.x*b.y-a.y*b.x);
+}
+__device__ __forceinline__ float3 vec3(const Vec3& a) {
+    return make_float3(a.x, a.y, a.z);
+}
+__device__ __forceinline__ float3 randomPoint(const Triangle& tri, unsigned& seed) {
+    seed ^= seed << 13; seed ^= seed >> 17; seed ^= seed << 5;
+    float u = static_cast<float>(seed >> 8) * 0x1p-24f;
+    seed ^= seed << 13; seed ^= seed >> 17; seed ^= seed << 5;
+    float v = static_cast<float>(seed >> 8) * 0x1p-24f;
+    if (u + v > 1.0f) { u = 1.0f-u; v = 1.0f-v; }
+    float3 a = vec3(tri.a);
+    return add3(a, add3(mul3(sub3(vec3(tri.b),a),u),
+                        mul3(sub3(vec3(tri.c),a),v)));
+}
+
+__device__ __forceinline__ bool rayBox(float3 p1, float3 p2, const DeviceNode& node) {
+    float3 d = mul3(sub3(p2,p1),0.5f);
+    float3 c = sub3(add3(p1,d),node.center);
+    float3 h = node.half;
+    float ax=fabsf(d.x), ay=fabsf(d.y), az=fabsf(d.z);
+    if (fabsf(c.x)>h.x+ax || fabsf(c.y)>h.y+ay || fabsf(c.z)>h.z+az) return false;
+    if (fabsf(d.y*c.z-d.z*c.y)>h.y*az+h.z*ay+EPSILON) return false;
+    if (fabsf(d.z*c.x-d.x*c.z)>h.z*ax+h.x*az+EPSILON) return false;
+    if (fabsf(d.x*c.y-d.y*c.x)>h.x*ay+h.y*ax+EPSILON) return false;
+    return true;
+}
+
+__device__ __forceinline__ bool rayTriangle(float3 orig, float3 dir,
+                                              const Triangle& tri, float maxDist) {
+    float3 v0=vec3(tri.a), e1=sub3(vec3(tri.b),v0), e2=sub3(vec3(tri.c),v0);
+    float3 pvec=cross3(dir,e2);
+    float det=dot3(e1,pvec);
+    if (fabsf(det)<EPSILON) return false;
+    float invDet=1.0f/det;
+    float3 tvec=sub3(orig,v0);
+    float u=dot3(tvec,pvec)*invDet;
+    if (u<0.0f || u>1.0f) return false;
+    float3 qvec=cross3(tvec,e1);
+    float v=dot3(dir,qvec)*invDet;
+    if (v<0.0f || u+v>1.0f) return false;
+    float dist=dot3(e2,qvec)*invDet;
+    return dist>EPSILON && dist<maxDist-EPSILON;
+}
+
+__device__ bool blocked(float3 p1, float3 p2, const Triangle* tris,
+                        const DeviceNode* nodes, const int* indices,
+                        int src, int dst) {
+    float3 d=sub3(p2,p1);
+    float len=sqrtf(dot3(d,d));
+    if (len<EPSILON) return true;
+    float3 dir=mul3(d,1.0f/len);
+    int nodeId=0;
+    while (nodeId<nodes[0].end) {
+        DeviceNode node=nodes[nodeId];
+        if (nodeId && !rayBox(p1,p2,node)) { nodeId=node.end; continue; }
+        for (int k=0;k<node.count;++k) {
+            int idx=indices[node.first+k];
+            if (idx!=src && idx!=dst && rayTriangle(p1,dir,tris[idx],len)) return true;
+        }
+        ++nodeId;
+    }
+    return false;
+}
+
+__global__ void precomputeKernel(const Triangle* tris, const DeviceNode* nodes,
+                                 const int* indices, float* kij, int* tau, int n) {
+    size_t pair=static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x)*blockDim.x+threadIdx.x;
+    size_t total=static_cast<size_t>(n)*n;
+    if (pair>=total) return;
+    int i=static_cast<int>(pair/n), j=static_cast<int>(pair%n);
+    if (i==j) { kij[pair]=0.0f; tau[pair]=0; return; }
+    const Triangle& a=tris[i];
+    const Triangle& b=tris[j];
+    float3 delta=sub3(mul3(add3(add3(vec3(a.a),vec3(a.b)),vec3(a.c)),1.0f/3.0f),
+                      mul3(add3(add3(vec3(b.a),vec3(b.b)),vec3(b.c)),1.0f/3.0f));
+    tau[pair]=static_cast<int>(ceilf(sqrtf(dot3(delta,delta))*INV_WAVE_SPEED));
+    float3 na=vec3(a._normal), nb=vec3(b._normal);
+    if (dot3(na,nb)>0.99f) { kij[pair]=0.0f; return; }
+    unsigned seed=static_cast<unsigned>(pair) ^ 0x9e3779b9u;
+    seed ^= seed >> 16; seed *= 0x7feb352du;
+    seed ^= seed >> 15; seed *= 0x846ca68bu;
+    seed ^= seed >> 16;
+    if (!seed) seed=1;
+    float sum=0.0f;
+    for (int r=0;r<NUM_RAYS;++r) {
+        float3 pI=randomPoint(a,seed), pJ=randomPoint(b,seed);
+        if (blocked(pI,pJ,tris,nodes,indices,i,j)) continue;
+        float3 v=sub3(pJ,pI);
+        float ds=dot3(v,v);
+        if (ds<EPSILON) continue;
+        float invLen=rsqrtf(ds);
+        float ci=fmaxf(0.0f,dot3(v,na)*invLen);
+        float cj=fmaxf(0.0f,-dot3(v,nb)*invLen);
+        if (ci>0.0f && cj>0.0f) sum+=(ci*cj)/(PI*ds);
+    }
+    kij[pair]=sum*INV_NUM_RAYS;
+}
+
+__global__ void propagateKernel(const float* kij, const int* tau, const float* area,
+                                float* radB, int n, int t, int source, int timeOff,
+                                float rho) {
+    int lane=threadIdx.x&31;
+    int i=(blockIdx.x*blockDim.x+threadIdx.x)>>5;
+    if (i>=n) return;
+    float sum=0.0f;
+    size_t row=static_cast<size_t>(i)*n;
+    for (int j=lane;j<n;j+=32) {
+        int delay=tau[row+j];
+        if (i==j || t<delay) continue;
+        float k=kij[row+j];
+        if (k<=0.0f) continue;
+        float rad=radB[static_cast<size_t>(t-delay)*n+j];
+        if (rad>0.0f) sum+=fminf(k*area[j],1.0f)*rad;
+    }
+    for (int offset=16;offset>0;offset>>=1)
+        sum+=__shfl_down_sync(0xffffffff,sum,offset);
+    if (lane==0) radB[static_cast<size_t>(t)*n+i]=rho*sum+(i==source && t<timeOff ? 1.0f : 0.0f);
+}
+
+__global__ void correlateKernel(const float* radB, float* corr, int n, int steps,
+                                int source) {
+    size_t pair=static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x)*blockDim.x+threadIdx.x;
+    if (pair>=static_cast<size_t>(n)*steps) return;
+    int i=static_cast<int>(pair/steps), lag=static_cast<int>(pair%steps);
+    float sum=0.0f;
+    for (int tt=lag;tt<steps;++tt)
+        sum+=radB[static_cast<size_t>(tt-lag)*n+source]*radB[static_cast<size_t>(tt)*n+i];
+    corr[pair]=sum;
+}
+
+__global__ void distanceKernel(const float* corr, float* distances, int n, int steps) {
+    int i=blockIdx.x*blockDim.x+threadIdx.x;
+    if (i>=n) return;
+    float best=0.0f;
+    int bestT=0;
+    for (int t=0;t<steps;++t) {
+        float v=corr[static_cast<size_t>(i)*steps+t];
+        if (v>best) { best=v; bestT=t; }
+    }
+    distances[i]=WAVE_SPEED*bestT;
+}
+
+struct GpuState {
+    Triangle* tris=nullptr;
+    DeviceNode* nodes=nullptr;
+    int* indices=nullptr;
+    float* area=nullptr;
+    float* kij=nullptr;
+    int* tau=nullptr;
+    float* radB=nullptr;
+    float* corr=nullptr;
+    float* distances=nullptr;
+    int n, steps, source;
+    float rho;
+
+    explicit GpuState(const SimulationState& state)
+        : n(static_cast<int>(state.numTriangles)),
+          steps(static_cast<int>(state.numTimesteps)),
+          source(static_cast<int>(state.sourceIndex)), rho(state.rho[0]) {
+        std::vector<DeviceNode> hostNodes;
+        std::vector<int> hostIndices;
+        flattenOctree(state.octree,hostNodes,hostIndices);
+        auto allocCopy=[](auto** dst, const auto& src) {
+            cudaCheck(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(dst),src.size()*sizeof(*src.data())));
+            cudaCheck(cudaMemcpy(*dst,src.data(),src.size()*sizeof(*src.data()),cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+        };
+        allocCopy(&tris,state.triangles);
+        allocCopy(&nodes,hostNodes);
+        allocCopy(&indices,hostIndices);
+        allocCopy(&area,state.areas);
+        size_t pairs=static_cast<size_t>(n)*n;
+        cudaCheck(cudaMalloc(&kij,pairs*sizeof(float)));
+        cudaCheck(cudaMalloc(&tau,pairs*sizeof(int)));
+        cudaCheck(cudaMalloc(&radB,static_cast<size_t>(n)*steps*sizeof(float)));
+        cudaCheck(cudaMemset(radB,0,static_cast<size_t>(n)*steps*sizeof(float)));
+        cudaCheck(cudaMalloc(&corr,static_cast<size_t>(n)*steps*sizeof(float)));
+        cudaCheck(cudaMalloc(&distances,static_cast<size_t>(n)*sizeof(float)));
+    }
+    ~GpuState() {
+        cudaFree(tris); cudaFree(nodes); cudaFree(indices); cudaFree(area);
+        cudaFree(kij); cudaFree(tau); cudaFree(radB); cudaFree(corr); cudaFree(distances);
+    }
+    void precompute() {
+        printf("Computing time delays (Tau)...\nComputing form factors (Kij)...\n");
+        size_t pairs=static_cast<size_t>(n)*n;
+        precomputeKernel<<<static_cast<unsigned>((pairs+255)/256),256>>>(tris,nodes,indices,kij,tau,n);
+        cudaCheck(cudaGetLastError());
+        cudaCheck(cudaDeviceSynchronize());
+    }
+    void simulate() {
+        printf("Running wave propagation simulation...\n");
+        for (int t=0;t<steps;++t) {
+            propagateKernel<<<(n*32+127)/128,128>>>(kij,tau,area,radB,n,t,source,steps/2,rho);
+            cudaCheck(cudaGetLastError());
+            if ((t+1)%10==0 || t+1==steps) printf("  Timestep %d/%d\n",t+1,steps);
+        }
+        cudaCheck(cudaDeviceSynchronize());
+    }
+    void computeDistances(SimulationState& state) {
+        printf("Computing distances via cross-correlation...\n");
+        size_t pairs=static_cast<size_t>(n)*steps;
+        correlateKernel<<<static_cast<unsigned>((pairs+255)/256),256>>>(radB,corr,n,steps,source);
+        cudaCheck(cudaGetLastError());
+        distanceKernel<<<(n+255)/256,256>>>(corr,distances,n,steps);
+        cudaCheck(cudaGetLastError());
+        cudaCheck(cudaDeviceSynchronize());
+        cudaCheck(cudaMemcpy(state.distances.data(),distances,n*sizeof(float),cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));
+        cudaCheck(cudaMemcpy(state.radB.data(),radB,pairs*sizeof(float),cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));
+        cudaCheck(cudaMemcpy(state.kij.data(),kij,static_cast<size_t>(n)*n*sizeof(float),cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));
+        cudaCheck(cudaMemcpy(state.tau.data(),tau,static_cast<size_t>(n)*n*sizeof(int),cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));
+    }
+};
+

 // Precomputation Phase

+// The CPU octree is flattened in depth-first order. A failed box test jumps
+// directly to the first node after the subtree, avoiding device recursion.
+struct DeviceNode {
+    float3 center, half;
+    int first, count, end;
+};
+
+static void flattenOctree(const Octree& node, std::vector<DeviceNode>& nodes,
+                          std::vector<int>& indices) {
+    int pos = static_cast<int>(nodes.size());
+    nodes.push_back({make_float3(node.center.x, node.center.y, node.center.z),
+                     make_float3(node.halfExtent.x, node.halfExtent.y, node.halfExtent.z),
+                     static_cast<int>(indices.size()),
+                     static_cast<int>(node.triangleIndices.size()), 0});
+    for (size_t idx : node.triangleIndices) indices.push_back(static_cast<int>(idx));
+    for (const auto& child : node.children) {
+        if (child) flattenOctree(*child, nodes, indices);
+    }
+    nodes[pos].end = static_cast<int>(nodes.size());
+}
+
+static void cudaCheck(cudaError_t status) {
+    if (status != cudaSuccess) {
+        fprintf(stderr, "CUDA error: %s\n", cudaGetErrorString(status));
+        std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);
+    }
+}
+
+__device__ __forceinline__ float3 add3(float3 a, float3 b) {
+    return make_float3(a.x+b.x, a.y+b.y, a.z+b.z);
+}
+__device__ __forceinline__ float3 sub3(float3 a, float3 b) {
+    return make_float3(a.x-b.x, a.y-b.y, a.z-b.z);
+}
+__device__ __forceinline__ float3 mul3(float3 a, float b) {
+    return make_float3(a.x*b, a.y*b, a.z*b);
+}
+__device__ __forceinline__ float dot3(float3 a, float3 b) {
+    return a.x*b.x + a.y*b.y + a.z*b.z;
+}
+__device__ __forceinline__ float3 cross3(float3 a, float3 b) {
+    return make_float3(a.y*b.z-a.z*b.y, a.z*b.x-a.x*b.z, a.x*b.y-a.y*b.x);
+}
+__device__ __forceinline__ float3 vec3(const Vec3& a) {
+    return make_float3(a.x, a.y, a.z);
+}
+__device__ __forceinline__ float3 randomPoint(const Triangle& tri, unsigned& seed) {
+    seed ^= seed << 13; seed ^= seed >> 17; seed ^= seed << 5;
+    float u = static_cast<float>(seed >> 8) * 0x1p-24f;
+    seed ^= seed << 13; seed ^= seed >> 17; seed ^= seed << 5;
+    float v = static_cast<float>(seed >> 8) * 0x1p-24f;
+    if (u + v > 1.0f) { u = 1.0f-u; v = 1.0f-v; }
+    float3 a = vec3(tri.a);
+    return add3(a, add3(mul3(sub3(vec3(tri.b),a),u),
+                        mul3(sub3(vec3(tri.c),a),v)));
+}
+
+__device__ __forceinline__ bool rayBox(float3 p1, float3 p2, const DeviceNode& node) {
+    float3 d = mul3(sub3(p2,p1),0.5f);
+    float3 c = sub3(add3(p1,d),node.center);
+    float3 h = node.half;
+    float ax=fabsf(d.x), ay=fabsf(d.y), az=fabsf(d.z);
+    if (fabsf(c.x)>h.x+ax || fabsf(c.y)>h.y+ay || fabsf(c.z)>h.z+az) return false;
+    if (fabsf(d.y*c.z-d.z*c.y)>h.y*az+h.z*ay+EPSILON) return false;
+    if (fabsf(d.z*c.x-d.x*c.z)>h.z*ax+h.x*az+EPSILON) return false;
+    if (fabsf(d.x*c.y-d.y*c.x)>h.x*ay+h.y*ax+EPSILON) return false;
+    return true;
+}
+
+__device__ __forceinline__ bool rayTriangle(float3 orig, float3 dir,
+                                              const Triangle& tri, float maxDist) {
+    float3 v0=vec3(tri.a), e1=sub3(vec3(tri.b),v0), e2=sub3(vec3(tri.c),v0);
+    float3 pvec=cross3(dir,e2);
+    float det=dot3(e1,pvec);
+    if (fabsf(det)<EPSILON) return false;
+    float invDet=1.0f/det;
+    float3 tvec=sub3(orig,v0);
+    float u=dot3(tvec,pvec)*invDet;
+    if (u<0.0f || u>1.0f) return false;
+    float3 qvec=cross3(tvec,e1);
+    float v=dot3(dir,qvec)*invDet;
+    if (v<0.0f || u+v>1.0f) return false;
+    float dist=dot3(e2,qvec)*invDet;
+    return dist>EPSILON && dist<maxDist-EPSILON;
+}
+
+__device__ bool blocked(float3 p1, float3 p2, const Triangle* tris,
+                        const DeviceNode* nodes, const int* indices,
+                        int src, int dst) {
+    float3 d=sub3(p2,p1);
+    float len=sqrtf(dot3(d,d));
+    if (len<EPSILON) return true;
+    float3 dir=mul3(d,1.0f/len);
+    int nodeId=0;
+    while (nodeId<nodes[0].end) {
+        DeviceNode node=nodes[nodeId];
+        if (nodeId && !rayBox(p1,p2,node)) { nodeId=node.end; continue; }
+        for (int k=0;k<node.count;++k) {
+            int idx=indices[node.first+k];
+            if (idx!=src && idx!=dst && rayTriangle(p1,dir,tris[idx],len)) return true;
+        }
+        ++nodeId;
+    }
+    return false;
+}
+
+__global__ void precomputeKernel(const Triangle* tris, const DeviceNode* nodes,
+                                 const int* indices, float* kij, int* tau, int n) {
+    size_t pair=static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x)*blockDim.x+threadIdx.x;
+    size_t total=static_cast<size_t>(n)*n;
+    if (pair>=total) return;
+    int i=static_cast<int>(pair/n), j=static_cast<int>(pair%n);
+    if (i==j) { kij[pair]=0.0f; tau[pair]=0; return; }
+    const Triangle& a=tris[i];
+    const Triangle& b=tris[j];
+    float3 delta=sub3(mul3(add3(add3(vec3(a.a),vec3(a.b)),vec3(a.c)),1.0f/3.0f),
+                      mul3(add3(add3(vec3(b.a),vec3(b.b)),vec3(b.c)),1.0f/3.0f));
+    tau[pair]=static_cast<int>(ceilf(sqrtf(dot3(delta,delta))*INV_WAVE_SPEED));
+    float3 na=vec3(a._normal), nb=vec3(b._normal);
+    if (dot3(na,nb)>0.99f) { kij[pair]=0.0f; return; }
+    unsigned seed=static_cast<unsigned>(pair) ^ 0x9e3779b9u;
+    seed ^= seed >> 16; seed *= 0x7feb352du;
+    seed ^= seed >> 15; seed *= 0x846ca68bu;
+    seed ^= seed >> 16;
+    if (!seed) seed=1;
+    float sum=0.0f;
+    for (int r=0;r<NUM_RAYS;++r) {
+        float3 pI=randomPoint(a,seed), pJ=randomPoint(b,seed);
+        if (blocked(pI,pJ,tris,nodes,indices,i,j)) continue;
+        float3 v=sub3(pJ,pI);
+        float ds=dot3(v,v);
+        if (ds<EPSILON) continue;
+        float invLen=rsqrtf(ds);
+        float ci=fmaxf(0.0f,dot3(v,na)*invLen);
+        float cj=fmaxf(0.0f,-dot3(v,nb)*invLen);
+        if (ci>0.0f && cj>0.0f) sum+=(ci*cj)/(PI*ds);
+    }
+    kij[pair]=sum*INV_NUM_RAYS;
+}
+
+__global__ void propagateKernel(const float* kij, const int* tau, const float* area,
+                                float* radB, int n, int t, int source, int timeOff,
+                                float rho) {
+    int lane=threadIdx.x&31;
+    int i=(blockIdx.x*blockDim.x+threadIdx.x)>>5;
+    if (i>=n) return;
+    float sum=0.0f;
+    size_t row=static_cast<size_t>(i)*n;
+    for (int j=lane;j<n;j+=32) {
+        int delay=tau[row+j];
+        if (i==j || t<delay) continue;
+        float k=kij[row+j];
+        if (k<=0.0f) continue;
+        float rad=radB[static_cast<size_t>(t-delay)*n+j];
+        if (rad>0.0f) sum+=fminf(k*area[j],1.0f)*rad;
+    }
+    for (int offset=16;offset>0;offset>>=1)
+        sum+=__shfl_down_sync(0xffffffff,sum,offset);
+    if (lane==0) radB[static_cast<size_t>(t)*n+i]=rho*sum+(i==source && t<timeOff ? 1.0f : 0.0f);
+}
+
+__global__ void correlateKernel(const float* radB, float* corr, int n, int steps,
+                                int source) {
+    size_t pair=static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x)*blockDim.x+threadIdx.x;
+    if (pair>=static_cast<size_t>(n)*steps) return;
+    int i=static_cast<int>(pair/steps), lag=static_cast<int>(pair%steps);
+    float sum=0.0f;
+    for (int tt=lag;tt<steps;++tt)
+        sum+=radB[static_cast<size_t>(tt-lag)*n+source]*radB[static_cast<size_t>(tt)*n+i];
+    corr[pair]=sum;
+}
+
+__global__ void distanceKernel(const float* corr, float* distances, int n, int steps) {
+    int i=blockIdx.x*blockDim.x+threadIdx.x;
+    if (i>=n) return;
+    float best=0.0f;
+    int bestT=0;
+    for (int t=0;t<steps;++t) {
+        float v=corr[static_cast<size_t>(i)*steps+t];
+        if (v>best) { best=v; bestT=t; }
+    }
+    distances[i]=WAVE_SPEED*bestT;
+}
+
+struct GpuState {
+    Triangle* tris=nullptr;
+    DeviceNode* nodes=nullptr;
+    int* indices=nullptr;
+    float* area=nullptr;
+    float* kij=nullptr;
+    int* tau=nullptr;
+    float* radB=nullptr;
+    float* corr=nullptr;
+    float* distances=nullptr;
+    int n, steps, source;
+    float rho;
+
+    explicit GpuState(const SimulationState& state)
+        : n(static_cast<int>(state.numTriangles)),
+          steps(static_cast<int>(state.numTimesteps)),
+          source(static_cast<int>(state.sourceIndex)), rho(state.rho[0]) {
+        std::vector<DeviceNode> hostNodes;
+        std::vector<int> hostIndices;
+        flattenOctree(state.octree,hostNodes,hostIndices);
+        auto allocCopy=[](auto** dst, const auto& src) {
+            cudaCheck(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(dst),src.size()*sizeof(*src.data())));
+            cudaCheck(cudaMemcpy(*dst,src.data(),src.size()*sizeof(*src.data()),cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+        };
+        allocCopy(&tris,state.triangles);
+        allocCopy(&nodes,hostNodes);
+        allocCopy(&indices,hostIndices);
+        allocCopy(&area,state.areas);
+        size_t pairs=static_cast<size_t>(n)*n;
+        cudaCheck(cudaMalloc(&kij,pairs*sizeof(float)));
+        cudaCheck(cudaMalloc(&tau,pairs*sizeof(int)));
+        cudaCheck(cudaMalloc(&radB,static_cast<size_t>(n)*steps*sizeof(float)));
+        cudaCheck(cudaMemset(radB,0,static_cast<size_t>(n)*steps*sizeof(float)));
+        cudaCheck(cudaMalloc(&corr,static_cast<size_t>(n)*steps*sizeof(float)));
+        cudaCheck(cudaMalloc(&distances,static_cast<size_t>(n)*sizeof(float)));
+    }
+    ~GpuState() {
+        cudaFree(tris); cudaFree(nodes); cudaFree(indices); cudaFree(area);
+        cudaFree(kij); cudaFree(tau); cudaFree(radB); cudaFree(corr); cudaFree(distances);
+    }
+    void precompute() {
+        printf("Computing time delays (Tau)...\nComputing form factors (Kij)...\n");
+        size_t pairs=static_cast<size_t>(n)*n;
+        precomputeKernel<<<static_cast<unsigned>((pairs+255)/256),256>>>(tris,nodes,indices,kij,tau,n);
+        cudaCheck(cudaGetLastError());
+        cudaCheck(cudaDeviceSynchronize());
+    }
+    void simulate() {
+        printf("Running wave propagation simulation...\n");
+        for (int t=0;t<steps;++t) {
+            propagateKernel<<<(n*32+127)/128,128>>>(kij,tau,area,radB,n,t,source,steps/2,rho);
+            cudaCheck(cudaGetLastError());
+            if ((t+1)%10==0 || t+1==steps) printf("  Timestep %d/%d\n",t+1,steps);
+        }
+        cudaCheck(cudaDeviceSynchronize());
+    }
+    void computeDistances(SimulationState& state) {
+        printf("Computing distances via cross-correlation...\n");
+        size_t pairs=static_cast<size_t>(n)*steps;
+        correlateKernel<<<static_cast<unsigned>((pairs+255)/256),256>>>(radB,corr,n,steps,source);
+        cudaCheck(cudaGetLastError());
+        distanceKernel<<<(n+255)/256,256>>>(corr,distances,n,steps);
+        cudaCheck(cudaGetLastError());
+        cudaCheck(cudaDeviceSynchronize());
+        cudaCheck(cudaMemcpy(state.distances.data(),distances,n*sizeof(float),cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));
+        cudaCheck(cudaMemcpy(state.radB.data(),radB,pairs*sizeof(float),cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));
+        cudaCheck(cudaMemcpy(state.kij.data(),kij,static_cast<size_t>(n)*n*sizeof(float),cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));
+        cudaCheck(cudaMemcpy(state.tau.data(),tau,static_cast<size_t>(n)*n*sizeof(int),cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));
+    }
+};

+// The CPU octree is flattened in depth-first order. A failed box test jumps
+// directly to the first node after the subtree, avoiding device recursion.
+struct DeviceNode {
+    float3 center, half;
+    int first, count, end;
+};
+
+static void flattenOctree(const Octree& node, std::vector<DeviceNode>& nodes,
+                          std::vector<int>& indices) {
+    int pos = static_cast<int>(nodes.size());
+    nodes.push_back({make_float3(node.center.x, node.center.y, node.center.z),
+                     make_float3(node.halfExtent.x, node.halfExtent.y, node.halfExtent.z),
+                     static_cast<int>(indices.size()),
+                     static_cast<int>(node.triangleIndices.size()), 0});
+    for (size_t idx : node.triangleIndices) indices.push_back(static_cast<int>(idx));
+    for (const auto& child : node.children) {
+        if (child) flattenOctree(*child, nodes, indices);
+    }
+    nodes[pos].end = static_cast<int>(nodes.size());
+}
+
+static void cudaCheck(cudaError_t status) {
+    if (status != cudaSuccess) {
+        fprintf(stderr, "CUDA error: %s\n", cudaGetErrorString(status));
+        std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);
+    }
+}
+
+__device__ __forceinline__ float3 add3(float3 a, float3 b) {
+    return make_float3(a.x+b.x, a.y+b.y, a.z+b.z);
+}
+__device__ __forceinline__ float3 sub3(float3 a, float3 b) {
+    return make_float3(a.x-b.x, a.y-b.y, a.z-b.z);
+}
+__device__ __forceinline__ float3 mul3(float3 a, float b) {
+    return make_float3(a.x*b, a.y*b, a.z*b);
+}
+__device__ __forceinline__ float dot3(float3 a, float3 b) {
+    return a.x*b.x + a.y*b.y + a.z*b.z;
+}
+__device__ __forceinline__ float3 cross3(float3 a, float3 b) {
+    return make_float3(a.y*b.z-a.z*b.y, a.z*b.x-a.x*b.z, a.x*b.y-a.y*b.x);
+}
+__device__ __forceinline__ float3 vec3(const Vec3& a) {
+    return make_float3(a.x, a.y, a.z);
+}
+__device__ __forceinline__ float3 randomPoint(const Triangle& tri, unsigned& seed) {
+    seed ^= seed << 13; seed ^= seed >> 17; seed ^= seed << 5;
+    float u = static_cast<float>(seed >> 8) * 0x1p-24f;
+    seed ^= seed << 13; seed ^= seed >> 17; seed ^= seed << 5;
+    float v = static_cast<float>(seed >> 8) * 0x1p-24f;
+    if (u + v > 1.0f) { u = 1.0f-u; v = 1.0f-v; }
+    float3 a = vec3(tri.a);
+    return add3(a, add3(mul3(sub3(vec3(tri.b),a),u),
+                        mul3(sub3(vec3(tri.c),a),v)));
+}
+
+__device__ __forceinline__ bool rayBox(float3 p1, float3 p2, const DeviceNode& node) {
+    float3 d = mul3(sub3(p2,p1),0.5f);
+    float3 c = sub3(add3(p1,d),node.center);
+    float3 h = node.half;
+    float ax=fabsf(d.x), ay=fabsf(d.y), az=fabsf(d.z);
+    if (fabsf(c.x)>h.x+ax || fabsf(c.y)>h.y+ay || fabsf(c.z)>h.z+az) return false;
+    if (fabsf(d.y*c.z-d.z*c.y)>h.y*az+h.z*ay+EPSILON) return false;
+    if (fabsf(d.z*c.x-d.x*c.z)>h.z*ax+h.x*az+EPSILON) return false;
+    if (fabsf(d.x*c.y-d.y*c.x)>h.x*ay+h.y*ax+EPSILON) return false;
+    return true;
+}
+
+__device__ __forceinline__ bool rayTriangle(float3 orig, float3 dir,
+                                              const Triangle& tri, float maxDist) {
+    float3 v0=vec3(tri.a), e1=sub3(vec3(tri.b),v0), e2=sub3(vec3(tri.c),v0);
+    float3 pvec=cross3(dir,e2);
+    float det=dot3(e1,pvec);
+    if (fabsf(det)<EPSILON) return false;
+    float invDet=1.0f/det;
+    float3 tvec=sub3(orig,v0);
+    float u=dot3(tvec,pvec)*invDet;
+    if (u<0.0f || u>1.0f) return false;
+    float3 qvec=cross3(tvec,e1);
+    float v=dot3(dir,qvec)*invDet;
+    if (v<0.0f || u+v>1.0f) return false;
+    float dist=dot3(e2,qvec)*invDet;
+    return dist>EPSILON && dist<maxDist-EPSILON;
+}
+
+__device__ bool blocked(float3 p1, float3 p2, const Triangle* tris,
+                        const DeviceNode* nodes, const int* indices,
+                        int src, int dst) {
+    float3 d=sub3(p2,p1);
+    float len=sqrtf(dot3(d,d));
+    if (len<EPSILON) return true;
+    float3 dir=mul3(d,1.0f/len);
+    int nodeId=0;
+    while (nodeId<nodes[0].end) {
+        DeviceNode node=nodes[nodeId];
+        if (nodeId && !rayBox(p1,p2,node)) { nodeId=node.end; continue; }
+        for (int k=0;k<node.count;++k) {
+            int idx=indices[node.first+k];
+            if (idx!=src && idx!=dst && rayTriangle(p1,dir,tris[idx],len)) return true;
+        }
+        ++nodeId;
+    }
+    return false;
+}
+
+__global__ void precomputeKernel(const Triangle* tris, const DeviceNode* nodes,
+                                 const int* indices, float* kij, int* tau, int n) {
+    size_t pair=static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x)*blockDim.x+threadIdx.x;
+    size_t total=static_cast<size_t>(n)*n;
+    if (pair>=total) return;
+    int i=static_cast<int>(pair/n), j=static_cast<int>(pair%n);
+    if (i==j) { kij[pair]=0.0f; tau[pair]=0; return; }
+    const Triangle& a=tris[i];
+    const Triangle& b=tris[j];
+    float3 delta=sub3(mul3(add3(add3(vec3(a.a),vec3(a.b)),vec3(a.c)),1.0f/3.0f),
+                      mul3(add3(add3(vec3(b.a),vec3(b.b)),vec3(b.c)),1.0f/3.0f));
+    tau[pair]=static_cast<int>(ceilf(sqrtf(dot3(delta,delta))*INV_WAVE_SPEED));
+    float3 na=vec3(a._normal), nb=vec3(b._normal);
+    if (dot3(na,nb)>0.99f) { kij[pair]=0.0f; return; }
+    unsigned seed=static_cast<unsigned>(pair) ^ 0x9e3779b9u;
+    seed ^= seed >> 16; seed *= 0x7feb352du;
+    seed ^= seed >> 15; seed *= 0x846ca68bu;
+    seed ^= seed >> 16;
+    if (!seed) seed=1;
+    float sum=0.0f;
+    for (int r=0;r<NUM_RAYS;++r) {
+        float3 pI=randomPoint(a,seed), pJ=randomPoint(b,seed);
+        if (blocked(pI,pJ,tris,nodes,indices,i,j)) continue;
+        float3 v=sub3(pJ,pI);
+        float ds=dot3(v,v);
+        if (ds<EPSILON) continue;
+        float invLen=rsqrtf(ds);
+        float ci=fmaxf(0.0f,dot3(v,na)*invLen);
+        float cj=fmaxf(0.0f,-dot3(v,nb)*invLen);
+        if (ci>0.0f && cj>0.0f) sum+=(ci*cj)/(PI*ds);
+    }
+    kij[pair]=sum*INV_NUM_RAYS;
+}
+
+__global__ void propagateKernel(const float* kij, const int* tau, const float* area,
+                                float* radB, int n, int t, int source, int timeOff,
+                                float rho) {
+    int lane=threadIdx.x&31;
+    int i=(blockIdx.x*blockDim.x+threadIdx.x)>>5;
+    if (i>=n) return;
+    float sum=0.0f;
+    size_t row=static_cast<size_t>(i)*n;
+    for (int j=lane;j<n;j+=32) {
+        int delay=tau[row+j];
+        if (i==j || t<delay) continue;
+        float k=kij[row+j];
+        if (k<=0.0f) continue;
+        float rad=radB[static_cast<size_t>(t-delay)*n+j];
+        if (rad>0.0f) sum+=fminf(k*area[j],1.0f)*rad;
+    }
+    for (int offset=16;offset>0;offset>>=1)
+        sum+=__shfl_down_sync(0xffffffff,sum,offset);
+    if (lane==0) radB[static_cast<size_t>(t)*n+i]=rho*sum+(i==source && t<timeOff ? 1.0f : 0.0f);
+}
+
+__global__ void correlateKernel(const float* radB, float* corr, int n, int steps,
+                                int source) {
+    size_t pair=static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x)*blockDim.x+threadIdx.x;
+    if (pair>=static_cast<size_t>(n)*steps) return;
+    int i=static_cast<int>(pair/steps), lag=static_cast<int>(pair%steps);
+    float sum=0.0f;
+    for (int tt=lag;tt<steps;++tt)
+        sum+=radB[static_cast<size_t>(tt-lag)*n+source]*radB[static_cast<size_t>(tt)*n+i];
+    corr[pair]=sum;
+}
+
+__global__ void distanceKernel(const float* corr, float* distances, int n, int steps) {
+    int i=blockIdx.x*blockDim.x+threadIdx.x;
+    if (i>=n) return;
+    float best=0.0f;
+    int bestT=0;
+    for (int t=0;t<steps;++t) {
+        float v=corr[static_cast<size_t>(i)*steps+t];
+        if (v>best) { best=v; bestT=t; }
+    }
+    distances[i]=WAVE_SPEED*bestT;
+}
+
+struct GpuState {
+    Triangle* tris=nullptr;
+    DeviceNode* nodes=nullptr;
+    int* indices=nullptr;
+    float* area=nullptr;
+    float* kij=nullptr;
+    int* tau=nullptr;
+    float* radB=nullptr;
+    float* corr=nullptr;
+    float* distances=nullptr;
+    int n, steps, source;
+    float rho;
+
+    explicit GpuState(const SimulationState& state)
+        : n(static_cast<int>(state.numTriangles)),
+          steps(static_cast<int>(state.numTimesteps)),
+          source(static_cast<int>(state.sourceIndex)), rho(state.rho[0]) {
+        std::vector<DeviceNode> hostNodes;
+        std::vector<int> hostIndices;
+        flattenOctree(state.octree,hostNodes,hostIndices);
+        auto allocCopy=[](auto** dst, const auto& src) {

+            cudaCheck(cudaMemcpy(*dst,src.data(),src.size()*sizeof(*src.data()),cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+        };
+        allocCopy(&tris,state.triangles);
+        allocCopy(&nodes,hostNodes);
+        allocCopy(&indices,hostIndices);
+        allocCopy(&area,state.areas);
+        size_t pairs=static_cast<size_t>(n)*n;
+        cudaCheck(cudaMalloc(&kij,pairs*sizeof(float)));
+        cudaCheck(cudaMalloc(&tau,pairs*sizeof(int)));
+        cudaCheck(cudaMalloc(&radB,static_cast<size_t>(n)*steps*sizeof(float)));
+        cudaCheck(cudaMemset(radB,0,static_cast<size_t>(n)*steps*sizeof(float)));
+        cudaCheck(cudaMalloc(&corr,static_cast<size_t>(n)*steps*sizeof(float)));
+        cudaCheck(cudaMalloc(&distances,static_cast<size_t>(n)*sizeof(float)));
+    }
+    ~GpuState() {
+        cudaFree(tris); cudaFree(nodes); cudaFree(indices); cudaFree(area);
+        cudaFree(kij); cudaFree(tau); cudaFree(radB); cudaFree(corr); cudaFree(distances);
+    }
+    void precompute() {
+        printf("Computing time delays (Tau)...\nComputing form factors (Kij)...\n");
+        size_t pairs=static_cast<size_t>(n)*n;
+        precomputeKernel<<<static_cast<unsigned>((pairs+255)/256),256>>>(tris,nodes,indices,kij,tau,n);
+        cudaCheck(cudaGetLastError());
+        cudaCheck(cudaDeviceSynchronize());
+    }
+    void simulate() {
+        printf("Running wave propagation simulation...\n");
+        for (int t=0;t<steps;++t) {
+            propagateKernel<<<(n*32+127)/128,128>>>(kij,tau,area,radB,n,t,source,steps/2,rho);
+            cudaCheck(cudaGetLastError());
+            if ((t+1)%10==0 || t+1==steps) printf("  Timestep %d/%d\n",t+1,steps);
+        }
+        cudaCheck(cudaDeviceSynchronize());
+    }
+    void computeDistances(SimulationState& state) {
+        printf("Computing distances via cross-correlation...\n");
+        size_t pairs=static_cast<size_t>(n)*steps;
+        correlateKernel<<<static_cast<unsigned>((pairs+255)/256),256>>>(radB,corr,n,steps,source);
+        cudaCheck(cudaGetLastError());
+        distanceKernel<<<(n+255)/256,256>>>(corr,distances,n,steps);
+        cudaCheck(cudaGetLastError());
+        cudaCheck(cudaDeviceSynchronize());
+        cudaCheck(cudaMemcpy(state.distances.data(),distances,n*sizeof(float),cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));
+        cudaCheck(cudaMemcpy(state.radB.data(),radB,pairs*sizeof(float),cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));
+        cudaCheck(cudaMemcpy(state.kij.data(),kij,static_cast<size_t>(n)*n*sizeof(float),cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));
+        cudaCheck(cudaMemcpy(state.tau.data(),tau,static_cast<size_t>(n)*n*sizeof(int),cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));
+    }
+};

+// The CPU octree is flattened in depth-first order. A failed box test jumps
+// directly to the first node after the subtree, avoiding device recursion.
+struct DeviceNode {
+    float3 center, half;
+    int first, count, end;
+};
+
+static void flattenOctree(const Octree& node, std::vector<DeviceNode>& nodes,
+                          std::vector<int>& indices) {
+    int pos = static_cast<int>(nodes.size());
+    nodes.push_back({make_float3(node.center.x, node.center.y, node.center.z),
+                     make_float3(node.halfExtent.x, node.halfExtent.y, node.halfExtent.z),
+                     static_cast<int>(indices.size()),
+                     static_cast<int>(node.triangleIndices.size()), 0});
+    for (size_t idx : node.triangleIndices) indices.push_back(static_cast<int>(idx));
+    for (const auto& child : node.children) {
+        if (child) flattenOctree(*child, nodes, indices);
+    }
+    nodes[pos].end = static_cast<int>(nodes.size());
+}
+
+static void cudaCheck(cudaError_t status) {
+    if (status != cudaSuccess) {
+        fprintf(stderr, "CUDA error: %s\n", cudaGetErrorString(status));
+        std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);
+    }
+}
+
+__device__ __forceinline__ float3 add3(float3 a, float3 b) {
+    return make_float3(a.x+b.x, a.y+b.y, a.z+b.z);
+}
+__device__ __forceinline__ float3 sub3(float3 a, float3 b) {
+    return make_float3(a.x-b.x, a.y-b.y, a.z-b.z);
+}
+__device__ __forceinline__ float3 mul3(float3 a, float b) {
+    return make_float3(a.x*b, a.y*b, a.z*b);
+}
+__device__ __forceinline__ float dot3(float3 a, float3 b) {
+    return a.x*b.x + a.y*b.y + a.z*b.z;
+}
+__device__ __forceinline__ float3 cross3(float3 a, float3 b) {
+    return make_float3(a.y*b.z-a.z*b.y, a.z*b.x-a.x*b.z, a.x*b.y-a.y*b.x);
+}
+__device__ __forceinline__ float3 vec3(const Vec3& a) {
+    return make_float3(a.x, a.y, a.z);
+}
+__device__ __forceinline__ float3 randomPoint(const Triangle& tri, unsigned& seed) {
+    seed ^= seed << 13; seed ^= seed >> 17; seed ^= seed << 5;
+    float u = static_cast<float>(seed >> 8) * 0x1p-24f;
+    seed ^= seed << 13; seed ^= seed >> 17; seed ^= seed << 5;
+    float v = static_cast<float>(seed >> 8) * 0x1p-24f;
+    if (u + v > 1.0f) { u = 1.0f-u; v = 1.0f-v; }
+    float3 a = vec3(tri.a);
+    return add3(a, add3(mul3(sub3(vec3(tri.b),a),u),
+                        mul3(sub3(vec3(tri.c),a),v)));
+}
+
+__device__ __forceinline__ bool rayBox(float3 p1, float3 p2, const DeviceNode& node) {
+    float3 d = mul3(sub3(p2,p1),0.5f);
+    float3 c = sub3(add3(p1,d),node.center);
+    float3 h = node.half;
+    float ax=fabsf(d.x), ay=fabsf(d.y), az=fabsf(d.z);
+    if (fabsf(c.x)>h.x+ax || fabsf(c.y)>h.y+ay || fabsf(c.z)>h.z+az) return false;
+    if (fabsf(d.y*c.z-d.z*c.y)>h.y*az+h.z*ay+EPSILON) return false;
+    if (fabsf(d.z*c.x-d.x*c.z)>h.z*ax+h.x*az+EPSILON) return false;
+    if (fabsf(d.x*c.y-d.y*c.x)>h.x*ay+h.y*ax+EPSILON) return false;
+    return true;
+}
+
+__device__ __forceinline__ bool rayTriangle(float3 orig, float3 dir,
+                                              const Triangle& tri, float maxDist) {
+    float3 v0=vec3(tri.a), e1=sub3(vec3(tri.b),v0), e2=sub3(vec3(tri.c),v0);
+    float3 pvec=cross3(dir,e2);
+    float det=dot3(e1,pvec);
+    if (fabsf(det)<EPSILON) return false;
+    float invDet=1.0f/det;
+    float3 tvec=sub3(orig,v0);
+    float u=dot3(tvec,pvec)*invDet;
+    if (u<0.0f || u>1.0f) return false;
+    float3 qvec=cross3(tvec,e1);
+    float v=dot3(dir,qvec)*invDet;
+    if (v<0.0f || u+v>1.0f) return false;
+    float dist=dot3(e2,qvec)*invDet;
+    return dist>EPSILON && dist<maxDist-EPSILON;
+}
+
+__device__ bool blocked(float3 p1, float3 p2, const Triangle* tris,
+                        const DeviceNode* nodes, const int* indices,
+                        int src, int dst) {
+    float3 d=sub3(p2,p1);
+    float len=sqrtf(dot3(d,d));
+    if (len<EPSILON) return true;
+    float3 dir=mul3(d,1.0f/len);
+    int nodeId=0;
+    while (nodeId<nodes[0].end) {
+        DeviceNode node=nodes[nodeId];
+        if (nodeId && !rayBox(p1,p2,node)) { nodeId=node.end; continue; }
+        for (int k=0;k<node.count;++k) {
+            int idx=indices[node.first+k];
+            if (idx!=src && idx!=dst && rayTriangle(p1,dir,tris[idx],len)) return true;
+        }
+        ++nodeId;
+    }
+    return false;
+}
+
+__global__ void precomputeKernel(const Triangle* tris, const DeviceNode* nodes,
+                                 const int* indices, float* kij, int* tau, int n) {
+    size_t pair=static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x)*blockDim.x+threadIdx.x;
+    size_t total=static_cast<size_t>(n)*n;
+    if (pair>=total) return;
+    int i=static_cast<int>(pair/n), j=static_cast<int>(pair%n);
+    if (i==j) { kij[pair]=0.0f; tau[pair]=0; return; }
+    const Triangle& a=tris[i];
+    const Triangle& b=tris[j];
+    float3 delta=sub3(mul3(add3(add3(vec3(a.a),vec3(a.b)),vec3(a.c)),1.0f/3.0f),
+                      mul3(add3(add3(vec3(b.a),vec3(b.b)),vec3(b.c)),1.0f/3.0f));
+    tau[pair]=static_cast<int>(ceilf(sqrtf(dot3(delta,delta))*INV_WAVE_SPEED));
+    float3 na=vec3(a._normal), nb=vec3(b._normal);
+    if (dot3(na,nb)>0.99f) { kij[pair]=0.0f; return; }
+    unsigned seed=static_cast<unsigned>(pair) ^ 0x9e3779b9u;
+    seed ^= seed >> 16; seed *= 0x7feb352du;
+    seed ^= seed >> 15; seed *= 0x846ca68bu;
+    seed ^= seed >> 16;
+    if (!seed) seed=1;
+    float sum=0.0f;
+    for (int r=0;r<NUM_RAYS;++r) {
+        float3 pI=randomPoint(a,seed), pJ=randomPoint(b,seed);
+        if (blocked(pI,pJ,tris,nodes,indices,i,j)) continue;
+        float3 v=sub3(pJ,pI);
+        float ds=dot3(v,v);
+        if (ds<EPSILON) continue;
+        float invLen=rsqrtf(ds);
+        float ci=fmaxf(0.0f,dot3(v,na)*invLen);
+        float cj=fmaxf(0.0f,-dot3(v,nb)*invLen);
+        if (ci>0.0f && cj>0.0f) sum+=(ci*cj)/(PI*ds);
+    }
+    kij[pair]=sum*INV_NUM_RAYS;
+}
+
+__global__ void propagateKernel(const float* kij, const int* tau, const float* area,
+                                float* radB, int n, int t, int source, int timeOff,
+                                float rho) {
+    int lane=threadIdx.x&31;
+    int i=(blockIdx.x*blockDim.x+threadIdx.x)>>5;
+    if (i>=n) return;
+    float sum=0.0f;
+    size_t row=static_cast<size_t>(i)*n;
+    for (int j=lane;j<n;j+=32) {
+        int delay=tau[row+j];
+        if (i==j || t<delay) continue;
+        float k=kij[row+j];
+        if (k<=0.0f) continue;
+        float rad=radB[static_cast<size_t>(t-delay)*n+j];
+        if (rad>0.0f) sum+=fminf(k*area[j],1.0f)*rad;
+    }
+    for (int offset=16;offset>0;offset>>=1)
+        sum+=__shfl_down_sync(0xffffffff,sum,offset);
+    if (lane==0) radB[static_cast<size_t>(t)*n+i]=rho*sum+(i==source && t<timeOff ? 1.0f : 0.0f);
+}
+
+__global__ void correlateKernel(const float* radB, float* corr, int n, int steps,
+                                int source) {
+    size_t pair=static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x)*blockDim.x+threadIdx.x;
+    if (pair>=static_cast<size_t>(n)*steps) return;
+    int i=static_cast<int>(pair/steps), lag=static_cast<int>(pair%steps);
+    float sum=0.0f;
+    for (int tt=lag;tt<steps;++tt)
+        sum+=radB[static_cast<size_t>(tt-lag)*n+source]*radB[static_cast<size_t>(tt)*n+i];
+    corr[pair]=sum;
+}
 
-    // Set source emission (active for first half of timesteps)
-    size_t timeOn = 0;
-    size_t timeOff = timesteps / 2;
-    for (size_t t = timeOn; t < timeOff; ++t) {
-        state.radE[state.idxTN(t, state.sourceIndex)] = 1.0f;
+__global__ void distanceKernel(const float* corr, float* distances, int n, int steps) {
+    int i=blockIdx.x*blockDim.x+threadIdx.x;
+    if (i>=n) return;
+    float best=0.0f;
+    int bestT=0;
+    for (int t=0;t<steps;++t) {
+        float v=corr[static_cast<size_t>(i)*steps+t];
+        if (v>best) { best=v; bestT=t; }
     }
+    distances[i]=WAVE_SPEED*bestT;
 }
 
+struct GpuState {
+    Triangle* tris=nullptr;
+    DeviceNode* nodes=nullptr;
+    int* indices=nullptr;
+    float* area=nullptr;
+    float* kij=nullptr;
+    int* tau=nullptr;
+    float* radB=nullptr;
+    float* corr=nullptr;
+    float* distances=nullptr;
+    int n, steps, source;
+    float rho;
+
+    explicit GpuState(const SimulationState& state)
+        : n(static_cast<int>(state.numTriangles)),
+          steps(static_cast<int>(state.numTimesteps)),
+          source(static_cast<int>(state.sourceIndex)), rho(state.rho[0]) {
+        std::vector<DeviceNode> hostNodes;
+        std::vector<int> hostIndices;
+        flattenOctree(state.octree,hostNodes,hostIndices);
+        auto allocCopy=[](auto** dst, const auto& src) {
+            cudaCheck(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(dst),src.size()*sizeof(*src.data())));
+            cudaCheck(cudaMemcpy(*dst,src.data(),src.size()*sizeof(*src.data()),cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+        };
+        allocCopy(&tris,state.triangles);
+        allocCopy(&nodes,hostNodes);
+        allocCopy(&indices,hostIndices);
+        allocCopy(&area,state.areas);
+        size_t pairs=static_cast<size_t>(n)*n;
+        cudaCheck(cudaMalloc(&kij,pairs*sizeof(float)));
+        cudaCheck(cudaMalloc(&tau,pairs*sizeof(int)));
+        cudaCheck(cudaMalloc(&radB,static_cast<size_t>(n)*steps*sizeof(float)));
+        cudaCheck(cudaMemset(radB,0,static_cast<size_t>(n)*steps*sizeof(float)));
+        cudaCheck(cudaMalloc(&corr,static_cast<size_t>(n)*steps*sizeof(float)));
+        cudaCheck(cudaMalloc(&distances,static_cast<size_t>(n)*sizeof(float)));
+    }
+    ~GpuState() {
+        cudaFree(tris); cudaFree(nodes); cudaFree(indices); cudaFree(area);
+        cudaFree(kij); cudaFree(tau); cudaFree(radB); cudaFree(corr); cudaFree(distances);
+    }
+    void precompute() {
+        printf("Computing time delays (Tau)...\nComputing form factors (Kij)...\n");
+        size_t pairs=static_cast<size_t>(n)*n;
+        precomputeKernel<<<static_cast<unsigned>((pairs+255)/256),256>>>(tris,nodes,indices,kij,tau,n);
+        cudaCheck(cudaGetLastError());
+        cudaCheck(cudaDeviceSynchronize());
+    }
+    void simulate() {
+        printf("Running wave propagation simulation...\n");
+        for (int t=0;t<steps;++t) {
+            propagateKernel<<<(n*32+127)/128,128>>>(kij,tau,area,radB,n,t,source,steps/2,rho);
+            cudaCheck(cudaGetLastError());
+            if ((t+1)%10==0 || t+1==steps) printf("  Timestep %d/%d\n",t+1,steps);
+        }
+        cudaCheck(cudaDeviceSynchronize());
+    }
+    void computeDistances(SimulationState& state, bool validation) {
+        printf("Computing distances via cross-correlation...\n");
+        size_t pairs=static_cast<size_t>(n)*steps;
+        correlateKernel<<<static_cast<unsigned>((pairs+255)/256),256>>>(radB,corr,n,steps,source);
+        cudaCheck(cudaGetLastError());
+        distanceKernel<<<(n+255)/256,256>>>(corr,distances,n,steps);
+        cudaCheck(cudaGetLastError());
+        cudaCheck(cudaDeviceSynchronize());
+        cudaCheck(cudaMemcpy(state.distances.data(),distances,n*sizeof(float),cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));
+        if (validation) {
+            cudaCheck(cudaMemcpy(state.radB.data(),radB,pairs*sizeof(float),cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));
+            cudaCheck(cudaMemcpy(state.kij.data(),kij,static_cast<size_t>(n)*n*sizeof(float),cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));
+        }
+    }
+};
+

+    return make_float3(a.x*b, a.y*b, a.z*b);
+}
+__device__ __forceinline__ float dot3(float3 a, float3 b) {
+    return a.x*b.x + a.y*b.y + a.z*b.z;
+}
+__device__ __forceinline__ float3 cross3(float3 a, float3 b) {
+    return make_float3(a.y*b.z-a.z*b.y, a.z*b.x-a.x*b.z, a.x*b.y-a.y*b.x);
+}
+__device__ __forceinline__ float3 vec3(const Vec3& a) {
+    return make_float3(a.x, a.y, a.z);
+}
+__device__ __forceinline__ float3 randomPoint(const Triangle& tri, unsigned& seed) {
+    seed ^= seed << 13; seed ^= seed >> 17; seed ^= seed << 5;
+    float u = static_cast<float>(seed >> 8) * 0x1p-24f;
+    seed ^= seed << 13; seed ^= seed >> 17; seed ^= seed << 5;
+    float v = static_cast<float>(seed >> 8) * 0x1p-24f;
+    if (u + v > 1.0f) { u = 1.0f-u; v = 1.0f-v; }
+    float3 a = vec3(tri.a);
+    return add3(a, add3(mul3(sub3(vec3(tri.b),a),u),
+                        mul3(sub3(vec3(tri.c),a),v)));
+}
+
+__device__ __forceinline__ bool rayBox(float3 p1, float3 p2, const DeviceNode& node) {
+    float3 d = mul3(sub3(p2,p1),0.5f);
+    float3 c = sub3(add3(p1,d),node.center);
+    float3 h = node.half;
+    float ax=fabsf(d.x), ay=fabsf(d.y), az=fabsf(d.z);
+    if (fabsf(c.x)>h.x+ax || fabsf(c.y)>h.y+ay || fabsf(c.z)>h.z+az) return false;
+    if (fabsf(d.y*c.z-d.z*c.y)>h.y*az+h.z*ay+EPSILON) return false;
+    if (fabsf(d.z*c.x-d.x*c.z)>h.z*ax+h.x*az+EPSILON) return false;
+    if (fabsf(d.x*c.y-d.y*c.x)>h.x*ay+h.y*ax+EPSILON) return false;
+    return true;
+}
+
+__device__ __forceinline__ bool rayTriangle(float3 orig, float3 dir,
+                                              const Triangle& tri, float maxDist) {
+    float3 v0=vec3(tri.a), e1=sub3(vec3(tri.b),v0), e2=sub3(vec3(tri.c),v0);
+    float3 pvec=cross3(dir,e2);
+    float det=dot3(e1,pvec);
+    if (fabsf(det)<EPSILON) return false;
+    float invDet=1.0f/det;
+    float3 tvec=sub3(orig,v0);
+    float u=dot3(tvec,pvec)*invDet;
+    if (u<0.0f || u>1.0f) return false;
+    float3 qvec=cross3(tvec,e1);
+    float v=dot3(dir,qvec)*invDet;
+    if (v<0.0f || u+v>1.0f) return false;
+    float dist=dot3(e2,qvec)*invDet;
+    return dist>EPSILON && dist<maxDist-EPSILON;
+}
+
+__device__ bool blocked(float3 p1, float3 p2, const Triangle* tris,
+                        const DeviceNode* nodes, const int* indices,
+                        int src, int dst) {
+    float3 d=sub3(p2,p1);
+    float len=sqrtf(dot3(d,d));
+    if (len<EPSILON) return true;
+    float3 dir=mul3(d,1.0f/len);
+    int nodeId=0;
+    while (nodeId<nodes[0].end) {
+        DeviceNode node=nodes[nodeId];
+        if (nodeId && !rayBox(p1,p2,node)) { nodeId=node.end; continue; }
+        for (int k=0;k<node.count;++k) {
+            int idx=indices[node.first+k];
+            if (idx!=src && idx!=dst && rayTriangle(p1,dir,tris[idx],len)) return true;
+        }
+        ++nodeId;
+    }
+    return false;
+}
+
+__global__ void precomputeKernel(const Triangle* tris, const DeviceNode* nodes,
+                                 const int* indices, float* kij, int* tau, int n) {
+    size_t pair=static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x)*blockDim.x+threadIdx.x;
+    size_t total=static_cast<size_t>(n)*n;
+    if (pair>=total) return;
+    int i=static_cast<int>(pair/n), j=static_cast<int>(pair%n);
+    if (i==j) { kij[pair]=0.0f; tau[pair]=0; return; }
+    const Triangle& a=tris[i];
+    const Triangle& b=tris[j];
+    float3 delta=sub3(mul3(add3(add3(vec3(a.a),vec3(a.b)),vec3(a.c)),1.0f/3.0f),
+                      mul3(add3(add3(vec3(b.a),vec3(b.b)),vec3(b.c)),1.0f/3.0f));
+    tau[pair]=static_cast<int>(ceilf(sqrtf(dot3(delta,delta))*INV_WAVE_SPEED));
+    float3 na=vec3(a._normal), nb=vec3(b._normal);
+    if (dot3(na,nb)>0.99f) { kij[pair]=0.0f; return; }
+    unsigned seed=static_cast<unsigned>(pair) ^ 0x9e3779b9u;
+    seed ^= seed >> 16; seed *= 0x7feb352du;
+    seed ^= seed >> 15; seed *= 0x846ca68bu;
+    seed ^= seed >> 16;
+    if (!seed) seed=1;
+    float sum=0.0f;
+    for (int r=0;r<NUM_RAYS;++r) {
+        float3 pI=randomPoint(a,seed), pJ=randomPoint(b,seed);
+        if (blocked(pI,pJ,tris,nodes,indices,i,j)) continue;
+        float3 v=sub3(pJ,pI);
+        float ds=dot3(v,v);
+        if (ds<EPSILON) continue;
+        float invLen=rsqrtf(ds);
+        float ci=fmaxf(0.0f,dot3(v,na)*invLen);
+        float cj=fmaxf(0.0f,-dot3(v,nb)*invLen);
+        if (ci>0.0f && cj>0.0f) sum+=(ci*cj)/(PI*ds);
+    }
+    kij[pair]=sum*INV_NUM_RAYS;
+}
+
+__global__ void propagateKernel(const float* kij, const int* tau, const float* area,
+                                float* radB, int n, int t, int source, int timeOff,
+                                float rho) {
+    int lane=threadIdx.x&31;
+    int i=(blockIdx.x*blockDim.x+threadIdx.x)>>5;
+    if (i>=n) return;
+    float sum=0.0f;
+    size_t row=static_cast<size_t>(i)*n;
+    for (int j=lane;j<n;j+=32) {
+        int delay=tau[row+j];
+        if (i==j || t<delay) continue;
+        float k=kij[row+j];
+        if (k<=0.0f) continue;
+        float rad=radB[static_cast<size_t>(t-delay)*n+j];
+        if (rad>0.0f) sum+=fminf(k*area[j],1.0f)*rad;
+    }
+    for (int offset=16;offset>0;offset>>=1)
+        sum+=__shfl_down_sync(0xffffffff,sum,offset);
+    if (lane==0) radB[static_cast<size_t>(t)*n+i]=rho*sum+(i==source && t<timeOff ? 1.0f : 0.0f);
+}
+
+__global__ void correlateKernel(const float* radB, float* corr, int n, int steps,
+                                int source) {
+    size_t pair=static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x)*blockDim.x+threadIdx.x;
+    if (pair>=static_cast<size_t>(n)*steps) return;
+    int i=static_cast<int>(pair/steps), lag=static_cast<int>(pair%steps);
+    float sum=0.0f;
+    for (int tt=lag;tt<steps;++tt)
+        sum+=radB[static_cast<size_t>(tt-lag)*n+source]*radB[static_cast<size_t>(tt)*n+i];
+    corr[pair]=sum;
+}
 
-    // Set source emission (active for first half of timesteps)
-    size_t timeOn = 0;
-    size_t timeOff = timesteps / 2;
-    for (size_t t = timeOn; t < timeOff; ++t) {
-        state.radE[state.idxTN(t, state.sourceIndex)] = 1.0f;
+__global__ void distanceKernel(const float* corr, float* distances, int n, int steps) {
+    int i=blockIdx.x*blockDim.x+threadIdx.x;
+    if (i>=n) return;
+    float best=0.0f;
+    int bestT=0;
+    for (int t=0;t<steps;++t) {
+        float v=corr[static_cast<size_t>(i)*steps+t];
+        if (v>best) { best=v; bestT=t; }
     }
+    distances[i]=WAVE_SPEED*bestT;
 }
 
+struct GpuState {
+    Triangle* tris=nullptr;
+    DeviceNode* nodes=nullptr;
+    int* indices=nullptr;
+    float* area=nullptr;
+    float* kij=nullptr;
+    int* tau=nullptr;
+    float* radB=nullptr;
+    float* corr=nullptr;
+    float* distances=nullptr;
+    int n, steps, source;
+    float rho;
+
+    explicit GpuState(const SimulationState& state)
+        : n(static_cast<int>(state.numTriangles)),
+          steps(static_cast<int>(state.numTimesteps)),
+          source(static_cast<int>(state.sourceIndex)), rho(state.rho[0]) {
+        std::vector<DeviceNode> hostNodes;
+        std::vector<int> hostIndices;
+        flattenOctree(state.octree,hostNodes,hostIndices);
+        auto allocCopy=[](auto** dst, const auto& src) {
+            cudaCheck(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(dst),src.size()*sizeof(*src.data())));
+            cudaCheck(cudaMemcpy(*dst,src.data(),src.size()*sizeof(*src.data()),cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+        };
+        allocCopy(&tris,state.triangles);
+        allocCopy(&nodes,hostNodes);
+        allocCopy(&indices,hostIndices);
+        allocCopy(&area,state.areas);
+        size_t pairs=static_cast<size_t>(n)*n;
+        cudaCheck(cudaMalloc(&kij,pairs*sizeof(float)));
+        cudaCheck(cudaMalloc(&tau,pairs*sizeof(int)));
+        cudaCheck(cudaMalloc(&radB,static_cast<size_t>(n)*steps*sizeof(float)));
+        cudaCheck(cudaMemset(radB,0,static_cast<size_t>(n)*steps*sizeof(float)));
+        cudaCheck(cudaMalloc(&corr,static_cast<size_t>(n)*steps*sizeof(float)));
+        cudaCheck(cudaMalloc(&distances,static_cast<size_t>(n)*sizeof(float)));
+    }
+    ~GpuState() {
+        cudaFree(tris); cudaFree(nodes); cudaFree(indices); cudaFree(area);
+        cudaFree(kij); cudaFree(tau); cudaFree(radB); cudaFree(corr); cudaFree(distances);
+    }
+    void precompute() {
+        printf("Computing time delays (Tau)...\nComputing form factors (Kij)...\n");
+        size_t pairs=static_cast<size_t>(n)*n;
+        precomputeKernel<<<static_cast<unsigned>((pairs+255)/256),256>>>(tris,nodes,indices,kij,tau,n);
+        cudaCheck(cudaGetLastError());
+        cudaCheck(cudaDeviceSynchronize());
+    }
+    void simulate() {
+        printf("Running wave propagation simulation...\n");
+        for (int t=0;t<steps;++t) {
+            propagateKernel<<<(n*32+127)/128,128>>>(kij,tau,area,radB,n,t,source,steps/2,rho);
+            cudaCheck(cudaGetLastError());
+            if ((t+1)%10==0 || t+1==steps) printf("  Timestep %d/%d\n",t+1,steps);
+        }
+        cudaCheck(cudaDeviceSynchronize());
+    }
+    void computeDistances(SimulationState& state, bool validation) {
+        printf("Computing distances via cross-correlation...\n");
+        size_t pairs=static_cast<size_t>(n)*steps;
+        correlateKernel<<<static_cast<unsigned>((pairs+255)/256),256>>>(radB,corr,n,steps,source);
+        cudaCheck(cudaGetLastError());
+        distanceKernel<<<(n+255)/256,256>>>(corr,distances,n,steps);
+        cudaCheck(cudaGetLastError());
+        cudaCheck(cudaDeviceSynchronize());
+        cudaCheck(cudaMemcpy(state.distances.data(),distances,n*sizeof(float),cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));
+        if (validation) {
+            cudaCheck(cudaMemcpy(state.radB.data(),radB,pairs*sizeof(float),cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));
+            cudaCheck(cudaMemcpy(state.kij.data(),kij,static_cast<size_t>(n)*n*sizeof(float),cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));
+        }
+    }
+};
+

+// The CPU octree is flattened in depth-first order. A failed box test jumps
+// directly to the first node after the subtree, avoiding device recursion.
+struct DeviceNode {
+    float3 center, half;
+    int first, count, end;
+};
+
+static void flattenOctree(const Octree& node, std::vector<DeviceNode>& nodes,
+                          std::vector<int>& indices) {
+    int pos = static_cast<int>(nodes.size());
+    nodes.push_back({make_float3(node.center.x, node.center.y, node.center.z),
+                     make_float3(node.halfExtent.x, node.halfExtent.y, node.halfExtent.z),
+                     static_cast<int>(indices.size()),
+                     static_cast<int>(node.triangleIndices.size()), 0});
+    for (size_t idx : node.triangleIndices) indices.push_back(static_cast<int>(idx));
+    for (const auto& child : node.children) {
+        if (child) flattenOctree(*child, nodes, indices);
+    }
+    nodes[pos].end = static_cast<int>(nodes.size());
+}
+
+static void cudaCheck(cudaError_t status) {
+    if (status != cudaSuccess) {
+        fprintf(stderr, "CUDA error: %s\n", cudaGetErrorString(status));
+        std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);
+    }
+}
+
+__device__ __forceinline__ float3 add3(float3 a, float3 b) {
+    return make_float3(a.x+b.x, a.y+b.y, a.z+b.z);
+}
+__device__ __forceinline__ float3 sub3(float3 a, float3 b) {
+    return make_float3(a.x-b.x, a.y-b.y, a.z-b.z);
+}
+__device__ __forceinline__ float3 mul3(float3 a, float b) {
+    return make_float3(a.x*b, a.y*b, a.z*b);

+__device__ __forceinline__ float3 cross3(float3 a, float3 b) {
+    return make_float3(a.y*b.z-a.z*b.y, a.z*b.x-a.x*b.z, a.x*b.y-a.y*b.x);
+}

+    return make_float3(a.x, a.y, a.z);

+__device__ __forceinline__ float3 randomPoint(const Triangle& tri, unsigned& seed) {
+    seed ^= seed << 13; seed ^= seed >> 17; seed ^= seed << 5;
+    float u = static_cast<float>(seed >> 8) * 0x1p-24f;
+    seed ^= seed << 13; seed ^= seed >> 17; seed ^= seed << 5;
+    float v = static_cast<float>(seed >> 8) * 0x1p-24f;
+    if (u + v > 1.0f) { u = 1.0f-u; v = 1.0f-v; }
+    float3 a = vec3(tri.a);
+    return add3(a, add3(mul3(sub3(vec3(tri.b),a),u),
+                        mul3(sub3(vec3(tri.c),a),v)));

+__device__ __forceinline__ bool rayBox(float3 p1, float3 p2, const DeviceNode& node) {
+    float3 d = mul3(sub3(p2,p1),0.5f);
+    float3 c = sub3(add3(p1,d),node.center);
+    float3 h = node.half;
+    float ax=fabsf(d.x), ay=fabsf(d.y), az=fabsf(d.z);
+    if (fabsf(c.x)>h.x+ax || fabsf(c.y)>h.y+ay || fabsf(c.z)>h.z+az) return false;
+    if (fabsf(d.y*c.z-d.z*c.y)>h.y*az+h.z*ay+EPSILON) return false;
+    if (fabsf(d.z*c.x-d.x*c.z)>h.z*ax+h.x*az+EPSILON) return false;
+    if (fabsf(d.x*c.y-d.y*c.x)>h.x*ay+h.y*ax+EPSILON) return false;
+    return true;
+}

+__device__ __forceinline__ bool rayTriangle(float3 orig, float3 dir,
+                                              const Triangle& tri, float maxDist) {
+    float3 v0=vec3(tri.a), e1=sub3(vec3(tri.b),v0), e2=sub3(vec3(tri.c),v0);
+    float3 pvec=cross3(dir,e2);
+    float det=dot3(e1,pvec);
+    if (fabsf(det)<EPSILON) return false;
+    float invDet=1.0f/det;
+    float3 tvec=sub3(orig,v0);
+    float u=dot3(tvec,pvec)*invDet;
+    if (u<0.0f || u>1.0f) return false;
+    float3 qvec=cross3(tvec,e1);
+    float v=dot3(dir,qvec)*invDet;
+    if (v<0.0f || u+v>1.0f) return false;
+    float dist=dot3(e2,qvec)*invDet;
+    return dist>EPSILON && dist<maxDist-EPSILON;
+}
+
+__device__ bool blocked(float3 p1, float3 p2, const Triangle* tris,
+                        const DeviceNode* nodes, const int* indices,
+                        int src, int dst) {
+    float3 d=sub3(p2,p1);
+    float len=sqrtf(dot3(d,d));
+    if (len<EPSILON) return true;
+    float3 dir=mul3(d,1.0f/len);
+    int nodeId=0;
+    while (nodeId<nodes[0].end) {
+        DeviceNode node=nodes[nodeId];
+        if (nodeId && !rayBox(p1,p2,node)) { nodeId=node.end; continue; }
+        for (int k=0;k<node.count;++k) {
+            int idx=indices[node.first+k];
+            if (idx!=src && idx!=dst && rayTriangle(p1,dir,tris[idx],len)) return true;
+        }
+        ++nodeId;
+    }
+    return false;
+}
+
+__global__ void precomputeKernel(const Triangle* tris, const DeviceNode* nodes,
+                                 const int* indices, float* kij, int* tau, int n) {
+    size_t pair=static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x)*blockDim.x+threadIdx.x;
+    size_t total=static_cast<size_t>(n)*n;
+    if (pair>=total) return;
+    int i=static_cast<int>(pair/n), j=static_cast<int>(pair%n);
+    if (i==j) { kij[pair]=0.0f; tau[pair]=0; return; }
+    const Triangle& a=tris[i];
+    const Triangle& b=tris[j];
+    float3 delta=sub3(mul3(add3(add3(vec3(a.a),vec3(a.b)),vec3(a.c)),1.0f/3.0f),
+                      mul3(add3(add3(vec3(b.a),vec3(b.b)),vec3(b.c)),1.0f/3.0f));
+    tau[pair]=static_cast<int>(ceilf(sqrtf(dot3(delta,delta))*INV_WAVE_SPEED));
+    float3 na=vec3(a._normal), nb=vec3(b._normal);
+    if (dot3(na,nb)>0.99f) { kij[pair]=0.0f; return; }
+    unsigned seed=static_cast<unsigned>(pair) ^ 0x9e3779b9u;
+    seed ^= seed >> 16; seed *= 0x7feb352du;
+    seed ^= seed >> 15; seed *= 0x846ca68bu;
+    seed ^= seed >> 16;
+    if (!seed) seed=1;
+    float sum=0.0f;
+    for (int r=0;r<NUM_RAYS;++r) {
+        float3 pI=randomPoint(a,seed), pJ=randomPoint(b,seed);
+        if (blocked(pI,pJ,tris,nodes,indices,i,j)) continue;
+        float3 v=sub3(pJ,pI);
+        float ds=dot3(v,v);
+        if (ds<EPSILON) continue;
+        float invLen=rsqrtf(ds);
+        float ci=fmaxf(0.0f,dot3(v,na)*invLen);
+        float cj=fmaxf(0.0f,-dot3(v,nb)*invLen);
+        if (ci>0.0f && cj>0.0f) sum+=(ci*cj)/(PI*ds);
+    }
+    kij[pair]=sum*INV_NUM_RAYS;
+}
+
+__global__ void propagateKernel(const float* kij, const int* tau, const float* area,
+                                float* radB, int n, int t, int source, int timeOff,
+                                float rho) {
+    int lane=threadIdx.x&31;
+    int i=(blockIdx.x*blockDim.x+threadIdx.x)>>5;
+    if (i>=n) return;
+    float sum=0.0f;
+    size_t row=static_cast<size_t>(i)*n;
+    for (int j=lane;j<n;j+=32) {
+        int delay=tau[row+j];
+        if (i==j || t<delay) continue;
+        float k=kij[row+j];
+        if (k<=0.0f) continue;
+        float rad=radB[static_cast<size_t>(t-delay)*n+j];
+        if (rad>0.0f) sum+=fminf(k*area[j],1.0f)*rad;
+    }
+    for (int offset=16;offset>0;offset>>=1)
+        sum+=__shfl_down_sync(0xffffffff,sum,offset);
+    if (lane==0) radB[static_cast<size_t>(t)*n+i]=rho*sum+(i==source && t<timeOff ? 1.0f : 0.0f);
+}
+
+__global__ void correlateKernel(const float* radB, float* corr, int n, int steps,
+                                int source) {
+    size_t pair=static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x)*blockDim.x+threadIdx.x;
+    if (pair>=static_cast<size_t>(n)*steps) return;
+    int i=static_cast<int>(pair/steps), lag=static_cast<int>(pair%steps);
+    float sum=0.0f;
+    for (int tt=lag;tt<steps;++tt)
+        sum+=radB[static_cast<size_t>(tt-lag)*n+source]*radB[static_cast<size_t>(tt)*n+i];
+    corr[pair]=sum;
+}
 
-    // Set source emission (active for first half of timesteps)
-    size_t timeOn = 0;
-    size_t timeOff = timesteps / 2;
-    for (size_t t = timeOn; t < timeOff; ++t) {
-        state.radE[state.idxTN(t, state.sourceIndex)] = 1.0f;
+__global__ void distanceKernel(const float* corr, float* distances, int n, int steps) {
+    int i=blockIdx.x*blockDim.x+threadIdx.x;
+    if (i>=n) return;
+    float best=0.0f;
+    int bestT=0;
+    for (int t=0;t<steps;++t) {
+        float v=corr[static_cast<size_t>(i)*steps+t];
+        if (v>best) { best=v; bestT=t; }
     }
+    distances[i]=WAVE_SPEED*bestT;
 }
 
+struct GpuState {
+    Triangle* tris=nullptr;
+    DeviceNode* nodes=nullptr;
+    int* indices=nullptr;
+    float* area=nullptr;
+    float* kij=nullptr;
+    int* tau=nullptr;
+    float* radB=nullptr;
+    float* corr=nullptr;
+    float* distances=nullptr;
+    int n, steps, source;
+    float rho;
+
+    explicit GpuState(const SimulationState& state)
+        : n(static_cast<int>(state.numTriangles)),
+          steps(static_cast<int>(state.numTimesteps)),
+          source(static_cast<int>(state.sourceIndex)), rho(state.rho[0]) {
+        std::vector<DeviceNode> hostNodes;
+        std::vector<int> hostIndices;
+        flattenOctree(state.octree,hostNodes,hostIndices);
+        auto allocCopy=[](auto** dst, const auto& src) {
+            cudaCheck(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(dst),src.size()*sizeof(*src.data())));
+            cudaCheck(cudaMemcpy(*dst,src.data(),src.size()*sizeof(*src.data()),cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+        };
+        allocCopy(&tris,state.triangles);
+        allocCopy(&nodes,hostNodes);
+        allocCopy(&indices,hostIndices);
+        allocCopy(&area,state.areas);
+        size_t pairs=static_cast<size_t>(n)*n;
+        cudaCheck(cudaMalloc(&kij,pairs*sizeof(float)));
+        cudaCheck(cudaMalloc(&tau,pairs*sizeof(int)));
+        cudaCheck(cudaMalloc(&radB,static_cast<size_t>(n)*steps*sizeof(float)));
+        cudaCheck(cudaMemset(radB,0,static_cast<size_t>(n)*steps*sizeof(float)));
+        cudaCheck(cudaMalloc(&corr,static_cast<size_t>(n)*steps*sizeof(float)));
+        cudaCheck(cudaMalloc(&distances,static_cast<size_t>(n)*sizeof(float)));
+    }
+    ~GpuState() {
+        cudaFree(tris); cudaFree(nodes); cudaFree(indices); cudaFree(area);
+        cudaFree(kij); cudaFree(tau); cudaFree(radB); cudaFree(corr); cudaFree(distances);
+    }
+    void precompute() {
+        printf("Computing time delays (Tau)...\nComputing form factors (Kij)...\n");
+        size_t pairs=static_cast<size_t>(n)*n;
+        precomputeKernel<<<static_cast<unsigned>((pairs+255)/256),256>>>(tris,nodes,indices,kij,tau,n);
+        cudaCheck(cudaGetLastError());
+        cudaCheck(cudaDeviceSynchronize());
+    }
+    void simulate() {
+        printf("Running wave propagation simulation...\n");
+        for (int t=0;t<steps;++t) {
+            propagateKernel<<<(n*32+127)/128,128>>>(kij,tau,area,radB,n,t,source,steps/2,rho);
+            cudaCheck(cudaGetLastError());
+            if ((t+1)%10==0 || t+1==steps) printf("  Timestep %d/%d\n",t+1,steps);
+        }
+        cudaCheck(cudaDeviceSynchronize());
+    }
+    void computeDistances(SimulationState& state, bool validation) {
+        printf("Computing distances via cross-correlation...\n");
+        size_t pairs=static_cast<size_t>(n)*steps;
+        correlateKernel<<<static_cast<unsigned>((pairs+255)/256),256>>>(radB,corr,n,steps,source);
+        cudaCheck(cudaGetLastError());
+        distanceKernel<<<(n+255)/256,256>>>(corr,distances,n,steps);
+        cudaCheck(cudaGetLastError());
+        cudaCheck(cudaDeviceSynchronize());
+        cudaCheck(cudaMemcpy(state.distances.data(),distances,n*sizeof(float),cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));
+        if (validation) {
+            cudaCheck(cudaMemcpy(state.radB.data(),radB,pairs*sizeof(float),cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));
+            cudaCheck(cudaMemcpy(state.kij.data(),kij,static_cast<size_t>(n)*n*sizeof(float),cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));
+        }
+    }
+};
